      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

筛选参数\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--format_threshold\\\", type=float, default=0.5,\\n                        help=\\\"format_reward 最低阈值 (default: 0.5)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--f1_threshold\\\", type=float, default=0.0,\\n                        help=\\\"f1_reward 最低阈值 (default: 0.0)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--ced_threshold\\\", type=float, default=0.0,\\n                        help=\\\"ced_reward 最低阈值 (default: 0.0)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--selection_strategy\\\", type=str, default=\\\"top_pct\\\",\\n                        choices=[\\\"threshold\\\", \\\"top_k\\\", \\\"top_pct\\\"],\\n                        help=\\\"筛选策略 (default: top_pct)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--top_k\\\", type=int, default=2,\\n                        help=\\\"top_k 策略: 每个文档取 top-K (default: 2)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--top_pct\\\", type=int, default=25,\\n                        help=\\\"top_pct 策略: 每个文档取 top N%% (default: 25)\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_path)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    # 1. 加载生成结果\\n    records = load_generations(args.input_dir)\\n\\n    # 2. 加载 CED 模型并打分\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading NLI model: %s\\\", args.nli_model_path)\\n    from freige.rewards.ced_reward import CEDRewardModel\\n    ced_model = CEDRewardModel(model_name=args.nli_model_path, device=args.nli_device)\\n\\n    scored = score_all_records(records, ced_model, tau=args.tau)\\n\\n    # 保存带分数的完整记录\\n    scores_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_scores.jsonl\\\"\\n    with open(scores_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for rec in scored:\\n            # 不保存大字段以节省空间\\n            slim = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in (\\\"prompt\\\", \\\"sents\\\", \\\"gold_triples\\\")}\\n            f.write(json.dumps(slim, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"Scores → %s\\\", scores_path)\\n\\n    # 3. 筛选\\n    selected = filter_records(scored, args)\\n\\n    # 4. 转换为 SFT 训练格式\\n    sft_data = format_sft_training_data(selected)\\n    train_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_train.json\\\"\\n    with open(train_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(sft_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Training data → %s (%d samples)\\\", train_path, len(sft_data))\\n\\n    # 5. 统计报告\\n    report = generate_report(scored, selected)\\n    report[\\\"args\\\"] = vars(args)\\n    report_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_report.json\\\"\\n    with open(report_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Report → %s\\\", report_path)\\n\\n    # 控制台摘要\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(f\\\"  Total records:    {report['total_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selected:         {report['selected_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selection rate:   {report['selection_rate']:.1%}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Unique docs:      {report['unique_docs_selected']}/{report['unique_docs_total']}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"-\\\" * 60)\\n    for name in [\\\"format_reward\\\", \\\"f1_reward\\\", \\\"ced_reward\\\", \\\"combined_score\\\"]:\\n        stats = report[f\\\"{name}_stats\\\"]\\n        print(f\\\"  {name:20s}  mean={stats['mean']:.3f}  std={stats['std']:.3f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"median={stats['median']:.3f}  [min={stats['min']:.3f}, max={stats['max']:.3f}]\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.37}}"

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"RSFT (Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning) 训练脚本。\\n\\n从 SFT warmup checkpoint 继续训练，使用经 CED 打分筛选的高质量生成数据。\\n流程: base model → merge SFT   skipped = 0\\n    for s in samples:\\n        messages = [\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": skipped:\\n        logger.warning(\\\"Skipped %d samples exceeding max_length=%d\\\", skipped, max_length)\\n\\n    if ced_scores:\\n        logger.info(\\n            \\\"Prepared %d RSFT samples Dataset.from_list(formatted), ced_scores\\n\\n\\nclass WeightedSFTTrainer(SFTTrainer):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"SFTTrainer with CED score-weighted sampling.\\n\\n    高 CED score 的样本被更频繁采样。使用 None:\\n            ds = dataset if dataset is not None else self.train_dataset\\n            return WeightedRandomSampler(\\n                weights=self.sample_weights,\\n                num_samples=len(ds),\\n                replacement=True,\\n            )\\n        return super()._get_train_sampler(dataset)\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = W&B\\n    if args.wandb_project and \\\"WANDB_API_KEY\\\" in os.environ:\\n        os.environ[\\\"WANDB_PROJECT\\\"] = args.wandb_project\\n        if args.wandb_run_name:\\n            os.environ[\\\"WANDB_NAME\\\"] = args.wandb_run_name\\n\\n    # Tokenizer\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading tokenizer: %s\\\", args.base_model)\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n        args.base_model, trust_remote_code=True, padding_side=\\\"right\\\",\\n    )\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    # Data\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading RSFT data: %s\\\", args.rsft_data_path)\\n    train_dataset, ced_scores = load_rsft_data(\\n        args.rsft_data_path, tokenizer, args.max_length,\\n    )\\n\\n    # Model: base → merge SFT adapter → new LoRA\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading base model: %s\\\", args.base_model)\\n    local_rank = int(os.environ.get(\\\"LOCAL_RANK\\\", 0))\\n    model_kwargs = dict(\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=\\\"sdpa\\\",\\n        device_map={\\\"\\\": local_rank},\\n    )\\n    if args.quantize:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Using 4-bit QLoRA quantization\\\")\\n        model_kwargs[\\\"quantization_config\\\"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(\\n            load_in_4bit=True,\\n            bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n            bnb_4bit_quant_type=\\\"nf4\\\",\\n            bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,\\n        )\\n    else:\\n        model_kwargs[\\\"torch_dtype\\\"] = torch.bfloat16 if args.bf16 else torch.float16\\n\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(args.base_model, **model_kwargs)\\n\\n    if args.sft_adapter:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Merging SFT adapter: %s\\\", args.sft_adapter)\\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.sft_adapter)\\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\\n\\n    model.config.use_cache = False\\n    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable()\\n\\n    # Fresh LoRA for RSFT (与 SFT warmup 相同结构)\\n    lora_config = LoraConfig(\\n        task_type=TaskType.CAUSAL_LM,\\n        r=args.lora_rank,\\n        lora_alpha=args.lora_alpha,\\n        lora_dropout=args.lora_dropout,\\n        target_modules=[\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                         \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\"],\\n    )\\n    model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)\\n    model.print_trainable_parameters()\\n\\n    # Training config\\n    training_args = SFTConfig(\\n        output_dir=args.output_dir,\\n        num_train_epochs=args.num_epochs,\\n        per_device_train_batch_size=args.per_device_batch_size,\\n        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,\\n        learning_rate=args.learning_rate,\\n        warmup_steps=args.warmup_steps,\\n        bf16=args.bf16,\\n        logging_steps=10,\\n        save_strategy=\\\"steps\\\",\\n        save_steps=args.save_steps,\\n        save_total_limit=args.save_total_limit,\\n        seed=args.seed,\\n        max_length=args.max_length,\\n        dataset_text_field=\\\"text\\\",\\n        report_to=\\\"wandb\\\" if \\\"WANDB_API_KEY\\\" in os.environ else \\\"none\\\",\\n        gradient_checkpointing=True,\\n        gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={\\\"use_reentrant\\\": False},\\n        deepspeed=args.deepspeed,\\n    )\\n\\n    sample_weights = ced_scores if args.weighted_sampling else None\\n\\n    trainer = WeightedSFTTrainer(\\n        model=model,\\n        args=training_args,\\n        train_dataset=train_dataset,\\n        processing_class=tokenizer,\\n        sample_weights=sample_weights,\\n    )\\n\\n    logger.info(\\n        \\\"Starting RSFT training (%d samples, weighted=%s)...\\\",\\n        len(train_dataset), args.weighted_sampling,\\n    )\\n    trainer.train()\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saving model to %s\\\", args.output_dir)\\n    trainer.save_model(args.output_dir)\\n    tokenizer.save_pretrained(args.output_dir)\\n    logger.info(\\\"RSFT training complete.\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.34}}"}

  \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    processor = DocREDProcessor(data_dir=data_dir)\\n    samples = processor.process(split)\\n    groups = processor.group_by_document(samples)\\n\\n    items = []\\n    for doc_id, {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"RSFT Step 2: CED 打分 + 筛选。\\n\\n读取 rsft_generate.py 生成的 JSONL，对每个候选输出计算三种奖励:\\n  - format_reward: JSON 格式合规性\\n  - f1_reward: 与 gold triples 的 micro-F1\\n  - ced_reward: CED 对比证据判别奖励\\n\\n按组合阈值筛选高质量样本，输出 SFT 训练兼容格式。\\n\\n输入:\\n  - generations.jsonl (rsft_generate.py 的输出)\\n\\n输出 (保存到 --output_path):\\n  - rsft_train.json: 筛选后的 SFT 训练数据\\n  - rsft_scores.jsonl: 带分数的完整记录（供分析）\\n  - rsft_report.json: 统计报告\\n\\n依赖: transformers, torch, freige.rewards.ced_reward\\n\\n用法:\\n  python -m freige.training.rsft_score_filter \\\\\\n      --input_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \\\\\\n      --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered \\\\\\n      --selection_strategy top_pct --top_pct 25 \\\\\\n      --nli_model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/nli-deberta-v3-base\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are an information extraction model. Given a document with numbered sentences \\\"\\n    \\\"and a list of entities, extract all relation triples. ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Reward functions — 复用 grpo_trainer 中的逻辑\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_format_reward(parsed_triples, format_ok):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"格式奖励，复用 grpo_trainer.format_reward_fn 的逻辑。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not format_ok:\\n        return 0.0\\n    if not parsed_triples:\\n        return 0.1\\n    valid = 0\\n    for item in parsed_triples:\\n        if (isinstance(item, dict) str(g.get(\\\"relation\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n            str(g.get(\\\"tail\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n        ))\\n\\n    pred_set = set()\\n    for p in parsed_triples:\\n \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n                str(p.get(\\\"tail\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n            ))\\n\\n    if not gold_set:\\n        return 1.0 if not pred_set else 0.0\\n\\n    tp = len(pred_set & gold_set)\\n    prec = tp / len(pred_set) if pred_set else 0.0\\n    rec = tp / len(gold_set) if gold_set else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else Now I'm looking at how `generate_batch` handles batching—it expands each prompt N times and generates all sequences in a single call, so with batch_size=2 and num_generations=8, that's 16 sequences processed together with the 4-bit quantized Qwen3-4 model.

On a 32GB RTX 5090, this could potentially cause OOM issues, though the code does have a fallback mechanism for that. The seed is set once at startup for temperature sampling, which means different batches get different random sequences as intended, but resuming from a checkpoint won't restore the RNG state, so reproducibility across resumed runs could be affected—though for RSFT this isn't critical since we just need diverse samples. The `generations.jsonl` file will also be quite large since each record stores the full prompt, gold triples, and sentences for all 3053 documents across 8 generations each.

Looking at the scoring filter now, the combined score formula has hardcoded weights (20% format, 40% F1, 40% CED) that aren't configurable through CLI arguments, which is problematic given that CED is supposed to be the core reward signal. The output formatting also relies on hardcoded Qwen3 chat template markers to extract user content, so switching models would break this extraction logic.

Now I'm examining a potential issue with the merge_and_unload() function when working with 4-bit quantized models—merging LoRA weights directly into quantized base weights could degrade quality, though the GRPO eval pipeline uses the same approach.

Actually, the PEFT library handles this by dequantizing before the merge and then re-quantizing afterward, so the quality impact should be minimal. What's interesting here is that after merging the SFT adapter, the code immediately applies a fresh LoRA configuration on top of the merged model. This creates an "SFT → RSFT" pipeline where you're training a new adapter on the already fine-tuned base model, which is a valid training strategy.

The weighted sampling implementation using WeightedRandomSampler with replacement looks correct for oversampling high-weight examples. Now I'm checking the LoRA configuration parameters...

Though there is a separate eval pipeline, having visibility into validation metrics during RSFT would be safer.

The gradient checkpointing configuration with `use_reentrant=False` is the right choice for current PyTorch versions.

Looking at the overall structure, the code is clean and follows the existing patterns well—the three-stage pipeline of generate, score/filter, then train is straightforward. But I'm noticing there's no import validation or dry-run test before deployment...

exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_r3\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: failed - KL explosion at step 5, batch=1 root cause\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_r4\\n    role: target\\n    result: pending approval\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D010\\n  date: 2026-05-14 12:32 UTC\\n  summary: 选择 Option A：保留 kl_coef=0.0 实验进程，修复 registry 状态从 killed → RUNNING；D009 的\\n    kl_coef=0.5 正式作废\\n  rationale: Reviewer critical 报告\\n\\n... <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Good, now I have the complete picture of the truncated section. The `compute_ced_reward_for_record` function is complete and correct. Let me now compile my review findings.

## Summary of Review

### Code structure
Three files deployed:
1. `rsft_generate.py` (296 lines) - Batch generation with temperature sampling
2. `rsft_score_filter.py` (453 lines) - CED scoring + multi-strategy filtering
3. `rsft_trainer.py` (252 lines) - SFT training on filtered data with optional weighted sampling


### Verified (PASS)
- All imports are valid: `DocREDProcessor` (group_by_document, format_sft_sample), `parse_model_output`, `CEDRewardModel`, `verbalize_triple`, `SYSTEM_PROMPT` from sft_trainer
- Package structure is consistent with existing code (same level as grpo_trainer, sft_trainer)
- `.pyc` files exist with timestamps matching the worker's runtime → modules were at least imported
- CED reward logic correctly implements F1 gate + per-triple CED scoring
- OOM fallback in generation
- Checkpoint/resume support in generation
- Weighted sampling in trainer

### Issues Found

1. **[

warning] Reward function duplication**: The format and F1 reward functions are duplicated from `grpo_trainer.py` rather than being imported, so any future changes to the reward logic won't propagate to the RSFT pipeline without manual updates.

2. **[warning] combined_score weights hardcoded**: The scoring formula blending format, F1, and CED rewards uses fixed weights that should be exposed as CLI arguments instead of being locked in the code.

3. **[warning] No RSFT tests written**: The test suite is missing coverage for the RSFT pipeline itself, with only the pre-existing CED gate tests present.

4. **[warning] `generate_batch` memory concern**: Expanding prompts by a factor of 8 and generating them all simultaneously could strain memory, especially with longer sequences. The OOM fallback helps, but still requires handling multiple concurrent generations.

5. **[info] No eval monitoring in RSFT trainer**: The trainer lacks a validation dataset for monitoring during training, which would be valuable given the project's prior issues with catastrophic forgetting.

6. **[info] SYSTEM_PROMPT duplicated**: The system prompt is defined separately in `rsft_score_filter.py` when it already exists elsewhere.

7. **[info] `rsft_generate.py` uses `device_map="auto"`**: This spreads the model across available GPUs, so I need to set `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` when running RSFT generation to avoid conflicts with other experiments.

The RSFT code itself doesn't violate GPU boundaries yet since it hasn't been executed as a training job, but the auto device mapping will require explicit GPU constraints when it runs.

Overall, the pipeline is structurally sound with a clean three-stage architecture, all dependencies check out, but there are gaps in functional testing and some hardcoded weights that should be addressed before production. I'd flag this as a warning — deployable but needs minor improvements.

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 — worker_ssh_rsft-code-verify\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### 审查范围\nWorker 在 rtx5090-4x 部署了 RSFT 三阶段流水线代码：\n- `rsft_generate.py` (296行) — 批量温度采样生成候选输出\n- `rsft_score_filter.py` (453行) — CED/F1/format 打分 + 多策略筛选\n- `rsft_trainer.py` (252行) — 基于筛选数据的 SFT 训练（支持 CED 加权采样）\n\n### 验证通过项\n- 所有外部依赖接口验证通过：`DocREDProcessor.group_by_document/format_sft_sample`、`parse_model_output`、`CEDRewardModel.compute_ced_reward`、`verbalize_triple`、`SYSTEM_PROMPT`\n- `.pyc` 文件时间戳 (07:25-07:59 UTC+8) 与 worker 运行时间吻合，确认模块至少完成了 import 编译\n- CED reward 逻辑正确实现了 F1 gate + per-triple CED 打分\n- OOM 回退机制（batch → single）、断点续传（progress.json）、加权采样（WeightedRandomSampler）均已实现\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: warning] **Reward 函数复制粘贴**: `compute_format_reward` 和 `compute_f1_reward` 从 grpo_trainer 复制而非导入。如果 reward 逻辑变更（例如 F1 匹配规则调整），RSFT pipeline 不会同步更新。应抽取到共享模块或直接 import。\n\n- [severity: warning] **combined_score 权重硬编码**: `rsft_score_filter.py` 中 `format*0.2 + f1*0.4 + ced*0.4` 写死在 `score_all_records()` 中，不可通过 CLI 调节。这是 RSFT 核心超参，实验时需要调优，建议暴露为 `--format_weight/--f1_weight/--ced_weight` 参数。\n\n- [severity: warning] **无功能测试**: tests/ 目录下无 RSFT 相关测试文件。Worker 仅验证了 import（pyc 编译通过），未做 dry-run 或 unit test。三个文件 1001 行代码，至少应有数据格式转换、筛选逻辑的基本测试。\n\n- [severity: warning] **generate_batch 显存风险**: batch_size=2 × num_generations=8 = 16 个序列同时生成（4096+1024 tokens），在 32GB RTX 5090 上可能 OOM。虽然有回退机制，但 single-prompt 回退仍是 8 并发序列。建议默认 batch_size=1 或 num_generations=4。\n\n- [severity: info] **rsft_generate 使用 device_map=\"auto\"**: 实际运行时必须设置 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` 约束 GPU，否则可能占用其他实验的 GPU。这是部署注意事项，不是代码 bug。\n\n- [severity: info] **RSFT trainer 无 eval 监控**: 只有 train_dataset，无 validation set。鉴于 GRPO 阶段反复出现 catastrophic forgetting，建议添加少量 dev 数据做 eval loss 监控。不过 RSFT 本质是 SFT，风险远低于在线 RL。\n\n- [severity: info] **rsft_score_filter.py 中 SYSTEM_PROMPT 未使用**: 定义了但从未引用，是 dead code。\n\n### GPU 边界\nRSFT 代码尚未作为实验运行，不存在当前 GPU 越界问题。当前 4 张 GPU 全部被 GRPO 收尾实验占用（cuda:0 训练 113/200, cuda:1/2/3 eval），符合 ROADMAP 描述。",

   batch_outputs, batch_trunc = generate_batch(\\n                    model, tokenizer, prompts,\\n                    args.num_generations, args.temperature, args.max_new_tokens,\\n                )\\n            except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError:\\n                logger.warning(\\\"OOM on batch of %d, falling back to one-by-one\\\", len(batch))\\n                torch.cuda.empty_cache()\\n batch_trunc.append(trunc[0])\\n\\n            for item, generations, trunc_flags in zip(batch, batch_outputs, batch_trunc):\\n                doc_triple_sets = set()\\n                for gen_idx,
